# Ultiself | Self-Improvement (App Store ID 1476563238) — Full Review Analysis

**Ultiself Corporation · `com.nekritin.ultiself` · Health & Fitness · free download, Premium subscription and separately sold plans**

Every one of the **4,511 reviews** in `reviews.jsonl` was read individually, in its original language, and hand-coded. Nothing was sampled, keyword-filtered or skipped. Corpus: **2020-05-12 → 2026-09-06**, **106 storefronts**, **25 languages** (English 2,171, Spanish 645, Russian 521, Ukrainian 261, French 243, German 196, Italian 110, Polish 107, and 17 more), corpus mean **3.55★**.

---

## How to read this

- Every quantified claim carries **count, percentage, denominator, scope, period, signal band and review IDs**. Signal bands are those in `Store Review Analysis Prompt.md`.
- Theme counts are **non-exclusive**: a review carrying five ideas is counted in five themes. Percentages are *shares of reviews mentioning a thing* and sum past 100%.
- **§10 is a complete per-review index.** Any number here resolves to exact reviews; any review resolves to the findings it supports via §9.J.
- Review IDs appear as `` `5936136499` `` (the `review_id` in `reviews.jsonl`) and as `#1` (position in date order, 1…4,511). Both appear in §10.
- Quotations are verbatim and short. Non-English text is quoted in the original with a translation. `…` marks an elision.
- Global percentages use **all 4,511 reviews**, from every storefront.

### ⚠️ Eight warnings before any number here is used

1. **Half the corpus is one six-month window with the marks of a review campaign.** E5 (2025-04 → 2025-09) holds 2,363 of 4,511 reviews (52.38%), averaging 394 a month against 99 in E4 and 103 in E6. It contains 230 of the 238 machine-pattern author names, 59 of the 60 reviews using a look-alike letter, and most cross-author duplicate bodies (§0.3, §9.H). Global praise shares are inflated by it; §7.6 recomputes every headline without E5.
2. **The ratings are bimodal and the text is harsher than the store.** 1★ 1,519 (33.67%), 5★ 2,693 (59.70%), 2–4★ together 299 (6.63%). Apple's lookup API (external, 2026-09-14) gives the US listing 4.33★ from 3,944 ratings; the US corpus mean is 3.73★, and 3.39★ outside E5.
3. **Most complaints are about purchases made outside the App Store.** Reviewers describe web, Apple Pay, PayPal and card billing that does not appear in their Apple subscriptions (`MON_WEB_BILLING` 303). The App Store review is where they complain, but many are not App Store subscribers. No purchase records exist in the data.
4. **Support praise in 2026 is partly solicited.** 45 E6 reviews name a support agent; several received refunds or free lifetime access, and one says the agent asked for the review (#4007). A 5★ review contains the line "Please post one review per day" (#3539). §0.4.
5. **Storefront is not language.** Ukraine is the second-largest storefront (474 reviews) and writes in Ukrainian, Russian and English; Israel's reviews are mostly one-line Hebrew 5★ from E5 (§6.15).
6. **Outcomes are self-reported** and coded as stated.
7. **Star and text sometimes disagree.** `CONTRA_RATING` **37** reviews, **0.82%** of 4,511; most are 5★ reviews reporting a charge (§4.3). They are coded by their text.
8. **Codes are hand judgement.** Each line in the classification file quotes the text behind its codes (§1.4); nothing was assigned by keyword or model. Authenticity flags in §9.H are mechanical and are never used to drop reviews.

# PART 0 — READ THIS FIRST

The eight findings that should change a decision.

## 0.1 Billing and cancellation is the dominant complaint, and it has one mechanism

`U_BILLING_COMPLAINT` **1434** reviews, **31.79%** of 4,511 — High-priority signal, mean 1.12★, 2021-02-28 → 2026-09-06. It is present in 1,384 of the 1,570 1–2★ reviews (88.2%). The parts: `MON_SCAM` **747** reviews, **16.56%** of 4,511; `MON_EXTRA_CHARGE` **632** reviews, **14.01%** of 4,511; `MON_CANCEL_HARD` **592** reviews, **13.12%** of 4,511; `MON_WEB_BILLING` **303** reviews, **6.72%** of 4,511; `MON_RENEWAL_SHOCK` **227** reviews, **5.03%** of 4,511; `MON_CHARGE_AFTER_CANCEL` **226** reviews, **5.01%** of 4,511; `MON_ESCALATE` **189** reviews, **4.19%** of 4,511.

Reviewers describe the same chain across 87 storefronts. The purchase is made on a website or through an in-funnel Apple Pay / PayPal / card payment; it does not appear in Apple's subscription list; the in-app "Manage subscription" button opens that list anyway (`BUG_MANAGE_SUB` **60** reviews, **1.33%** of 4,511); cancellation is by email only (`MON_EMAIL` **13** reviews, **0.29%** of 4,511); support answers with offers (`SUP_RETENTION` **85** reviews, **1.88%** of 4,511) or not at all (`SUP_NONE` **352** reviews, **7.80%** of 4,511); the charge recurs, and the reviewer blocks the card or disputes it with the bank. 207 reviews (4.59%) state both the web billing and the failure to stop it.

> "button directs to the App Store, but the actual subscription is handled outside via their website, to which access is blocked" — #1107, `12588412857`, ua, 1★

> "DOES NOT show their active subscription" — #3322, `13269087947`, by, 1★

> "В AppStore вы не увидите эту подписку" — #4093, `14060777661`, by, 3★
> *(translation: You won't see this subscription in the App Store)*

> "tuve que llamar al banco para dar orden de no pago" — #4415, `14402512848`, cl, 1★
> *(translation: I had to call the bank to order non-payment)*

#493 (8 votes) and #3322 (7 votes), both from Belarus, are the most-voted complaints in the corpus. The listing states "Subscription payments are charged to your iTunes account" (§2.2); the reviews describe payments that were not.

**Evidence:** `10422103648`, `12025277510`, `12327199496`, `12643318907`, `12782491881`, `12937648714`, `13089694166`, `13392825084`, `13769606347`, `14489567854`.

## 0.2 The purchase funnel sells on the next screen: a small plan, then $57

`U_FUNNEL_SELL` **178** reviews, **3.95%** of 4,511 — Very strong signal, mean 1.30★, 2021-12-20 → 2026-08-22. Its core is `MON_ONBOARD_SELL` **37** reviews, **0.82%** of 4,511: offers shown during sign-up or right after payment, where a tap adds a charge. The number **57** appears in 60 reviews (1.33%), with or without a currency symbol, 58 of them describing an unrequested extra charge, from 2024-06-12 → 2026-09-01 (§9.G lists the amounts written with a currency marker). The intro price is usually $6–10 for a week or a month. Separately, `MON_RENEWAL_SHOCK` **227** reviews, **5.03%** of 4,511 describe an intro plan that became a $39.95–$87 recurring charge, and `MON_BAIT` **55** reviews, **1.22%** of 4,511 describe an advertised price that was not the price charged.

> "along with the $9.92 charge, another $57 has been taken" — #4369, `14361404522`, gb, 1★

> "AUTOMATIQUEMENT vous venez d'acheter 3 PROGRAMMES SUPPLÉMENTAIRES, sans confirmation bancaire" — #4306, `14297661088`, ch, 1★
> *(translation: AUTOMATICALLY you have just bought 3 EXTRA PROGRAMS, without bank confirmation)*

> "Кликнул на кнопку списало еще 57$ кликнул на другую кнопку еще 28$" — #826, `12418199607`, ua, 1★
> *(translation: Clicked a button, another $57 was charged; clicked another button, another $28)*

> "no I do not wish to improve my health" — #4450, `14453772060`, au, 1★

#4450 describes the decline button on the add-on offer as worded to shame the user (`NEG_DARK_PATTERN`). The in-app upsell complaint is older and steadier: `MON_UPSELL` **80** reviews, **1.77%** of 4,511, from 2021-12-20 → 2026-08-22; paying for "premium" or "lifetime" and then being sold more is `MON_TIER_TRAP` **24** reviews, **0.53%** of 4,511, highest in E2 (4.6%).

**Evidence:** `8779339766`, `12232198948`, `12696482316`, `12785229691`, `12949399081`, `13433781575`, `13600668706`, `13815620846`, `14220380702`, `14497286745`.

## 0.3 The 2025 five-star record is not organic evidence

E5 holds 2,363 reviews in six months (394 a month) with a mean of 4.03★. The four months before average 2.23★ and the twelve months after 3.34★. Five mechanical marks concentrate in E5 (§9.H):

| Mark | Reviews | In E5 | 5★ | Reading |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| Author name with a birth-year-plus-letters suffix or a random consonant tail (e.g. `nashayla*mogenot@1987qmnp`, `Roswell Kolinlxvklluz`) | 238 | 230 | 236 | generated account names |
| Body uses the look-alike letter "ọ" (U+1ECD) inside ordinary words | 60 | 59 | 60 | text altered to evade duplicate detection |
| Body identical to another author's body (≥20 letters) | 56 in 28 groups | 38 | 55 | shared templates |
| Hebrew-language review | 52 | 49 | 51 | one-line 5★ from il |
| 5★ body of 80 characters or fewer | 1,885 | 1,544 | 1,885 | 87.4% of E5 5★ against 30.3% of E6 5★ |

> "Es como compita que siempre está" — #2146, `12920984302`, mx, 5★
> *(translation: It's like a buddy who is always there)*

> "Es como compita que siempre está" — #2150, `12921016298`, mx, 5★
> *(translation: It's like a buddy who is always there)*

> "ishlatish yoqimli" — #2557, `13019377855`, uz, 5★
> *(translation: pleasant to use)*

> "ishlatish yoqimli" — #2562, `13019421337`, uz, 5★
> *(translation: pleasant to use)*

#2146 and #2150 were posted on the same day in Mexico by `Cheryln Kettlesingdmtptwre` and `brealyn-todhunteraasmbcx`; #2557 and #2562 on the same day in Uzbekistan by `ismelda*southers-1993` and `roan@plaide 1984cggr`. Earlier, three pairs of long US 5★ reviews with identical text under different names appeared in 2022-10 → 2023-01 (#84/#114, #86/#119, #90/#159). `REV_DISTRUST` **8** reviews, **0.18%** of 4,511 say the positive reviews cannot be trusted.

**What this changes.** The E5 reviews are kept, read and coded like any others, but the public rating is not evidence of satisfaction: 5★ is 74.8% of E5 and 43.1% of everything else. §7.6 shows that without E5 the corpus is 41.8% billing complaint and 47.3% praise.

**Evidence (author-name flags, spread):** `11798385032`, `12665098547`, `12745933474`, `12794832009`, `12885150244`, `12977176172`, `13019421337`, `13090485917`, `13152464916`, `13203883959`.

## 0.4 Support moved from retention offers to named agents, and the praise is partly requested

Until 2025, support is described as silent or as answering a cancellation request with an offer to stay: `SUP_RETENTION` 55 reviews before 2025-10, `SUP_NONE` 273. In E6 (2025-10 →) support praise appears at scale: `PR_SUPPORT` 62 of 1,238 E6 reviews (5.0%) against 9 in the 3,273 reviews before. `U_NAMED_AGENT` **49** reviews, **1.09%** of 4,511 — Meaningful signal, mean 4.49★, 2023-02-17 → 2026-09-03: "Nick" (29), then "Emma", "Michael" and "Sam" (`SUP_NAMED_AGENT` 20, all 2026).

The same named-agent reviews show what earned the praise: 14 of 45 E6 named-agent reviews report a refund, a partial refund, free lifetime access or another retention gift. Two texts say reviews are being requested: "My feedback goes to Michael who asked me to write it" (#4007, `13977689947`, ee, 3★); "Please post one review per day" (#3539, `13518285285`, ng, 5★). The earlier pattern is visible in the same agent's name: "Nick keeps giving me offers I simply don't want" (#2500, `13005997779`, by, 1★).

> "Nick responded within hours. He canceled my subscription, issued a refund, and even upgraded me to lifetime access" — #4019, `13995164939`, by, 5★

> "have lifetime access to Ulitself and all the upgraded packages at no extra cost" — #3857, `13818735180`, gb, 5★

**All named-agent IDs:** #177 #2449 #2500 #3052 #3803 #3804 #3857 #3867 #3870 #3888 #3890 #3893 #3897 #3902 #3906 #3914 #3936 #3941 #3950 #3958 #3966 #4007 #4013 #4019 #4028 #4030 #4031 #4033 #4048 #4054 #4069 #4072 #4093 #4094 #4106 #4110 #4111 #4132 #4143 #4157 #4161 #4173 #4177 #4182 #4207 #4245 #4246 #4267 #4501.

## 0.5 Ads for quitting drinking, smoking and vaping sell a product the app is not

`USE_ADDICTION` **24** reviews, **0.53%** of 4,511 — Emerging signal, mean 3.21★, 2025-03-01 → 2026-09-01; 23 of them are from E6. The low-star ones (9) describe arriving from an Instagram or Facebook ad about alcohol, smoking or vaping (`MKT_AD` 5 state the ad), paying, and finding a general habit checklist (`NEG_NOT_AS_ADVERTISED` 5); the ad-to-product gap overall is `NEG_NOT_AS_ADVERTISED` **59** reviews, **1.31%** of 4,511. The positive addiction reviews (14) are short.

## 0.6 What genuinely works is a simple, attractive habit tracker

Outside E5, 1,015 of 2,148 reviews (47.3%) praise something, and the praise is specific: ease of use (`PR_EASY` 255), design (`PR_DESIGN` 148), reminders (`PR_REMINDERS` 68), routines (`PR_ROUTINES` 78), tracking (`PR_TRACKING` 119), the habit library (`PR_LIBRARY` 90) and the insights (`PR_INSIGHTS` 63). `PR_FREE` **26** reviews, **0.58%** of 4,511 say the free version is enough. Outcomes are reported across the corpus, `U_OUTCOME` **520** reviews, **11.53%** of 4,511 — High-priority signal, mean 4.95★, 2021-01-04 → 2026-09-01; 200 of them outside E5.

> "Best habit tracker I've come across" — #228, `10004391517`, us, 5★

> "Free Version is Great" — #229, `10005041599`, us, 4★

> "even just getting out of bed seemed like an incredibly daunting task" — #376, `11152761066`, ua, 5★

> "I find the basic non-paid version to be plenty" — #4001, `13973006077`, hu, 4★

#228 and #230 (19 votes each, June 2023) are the most-voted reviews in the corpus; both are short tracker praise. #376 (4 votes) credits the reminders with getting the reviewer out of bed.

**Evidence:** `5936136499`, `10879884153`, `11534120616`, `13218674682`, `13562938507`, `13718724538`, `13979155117`, `14159425095`, `14336306557`, `14507738284`.

## 0.7 For the price, the content reads as thin, generic and often in English

`NEG_SIMPLE` **97** reviews, **2.15%** of 4,511 — Meaningful signal, mean 1.40★, 2021-09-11 → 2026-08-21: "just a checklist", nothing beyond other apps or common knowledge. With generic content (`NEG_CONTENT` **33** reviews, **0.73%** of 4,511), personalisation that feels generic (`NEG_AI_POOR` **23** reviews, **0.51%** of 4,511) and objections to AI-generated content (`NEG_AI_CONTENT` **8** reviews, **0.18%** of 4,511, all E6), 137 reviews (3.04%) find the product thin; 32 of them also object to price. `NEG_LANG` **32** reviews, **0.71%** of 4,511 report an interface in their language with videos, notifications or the whole app in English, often after an ad in their language.

# PART 1 — SCOPE AND METHOD

## 1.1 Files used

| File | Role | Records |
|---|---|---:|
| `App Store Reviews/68. Ultiself - Self-Improvement - Biohacker Routine Planner App/reviews.jsonl` | **the corpus**; every claim resolves here | 4,511 |
| `by_country/*.jsonl` | 106 per-storefront files | 4,511 (union) |
| `manifest.json` | extraction metadata, per-country counts, rating distribution | — |
| `_state.json` | collection state per polled storefront | — |
| Apple iTunes Lookup API (external, 2026-09-14) | public rating, listing text, version, languages | 20 storefronts |

## 1.2 Schema actually present

All 13 fields are present on all 4,511 records; no title or body is empty.

| Field | Used for | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `review_id` | primary key | 4,511 distinct; zero duplicates |
| `country`, `country_name` | §6 | 106 storefronts |
| `rating` | §4 | integers 1–5 only |
| `title`, `body` | **read in full for every record** | median body length 82 characters |
| `author` | authenticity tests only (§9.H) | 7 names appear on more than one storefront |
| `date` | §7 | 2020-05-12 → 2026-09-06 |
| `vote_count`, `vote_sum` | §9.H | 212 reviews have votes; max 19 |
| `is_edited` | §9.H | 163 edited |
| `app_id`, `app_name` | constant (`1476563238`, `Ultiself | Self-Improvement`) | |

**Not present, and therefore never claimed:** app version per review, purchase or billing records, developer responses, device, and any identifier linking a reviewer across storefronts or to a purchase.

## 1.3 Coverage and reconciliation

`68-reconcile.py` output, verbatim:

```

corpus mean rating           : 3.5480  manifest 3.548  match=True
_state storefronts probed    : 139
_state all complete          : True

_state zero-review fronts    : 33
sum _state collected         : 4511
field presence               : {'review_id': 4511, 'app_id': 4511, 'app_name': 4511, 'country': 4511, 'country_name': 4511, 'rating': 4511, 'title': 4511, 'body': 4511, 'author': 4511, 'date': 4511, 'vote_count': 4511, 'vote_sum': 4511, 'is_edited': 4511}
empty title / body           : 0 / 0
ratings outside 1-5          : 0
date range                   : 2020-05-12T14:59:17Z -> 2026-09-06T14:45:57Z

is_edited true               : 163
vote_count>0                 : 212, max 19

```

**Reviews read: 4,511/4,511 = 100%. Reviews hand-coded: 4,511/4,511 = 100%.** No deduplication was applied: there are no technical duplicates. Identical bodies under different authors (56 reviews in 28 groups) are kept and disclosed (§9.H).

## 1.4 Processing method

1. `68-dump.py` emits all 4,511 reviews in date order with a stable index (1…4,511).
2. Reviews were read in date order in batches of 100 (the last 45 classification files cover them, the final one holding 111), in full, in the original language. Translations quoted here were checked against the source string.
3. Each review received hand codes plus a note quoting the text that drove them (`68-cls/*.txt`, 4,511 lines).
4. **After every batch**, `68-check-cls.py` re-asserted each line's `review_id` at its index and that every quoted fragment in the note occurs in that review. Lines were written without IDs and `68-fill-ids.py` filled them from the index, so an ID cannot drift from its text (§9.C).
5. `68-build-classification.py` rejects unknown codes, adds mechanical codes (`STAR_n`, `ERA_En`) and derives 16 unions.
6. `68-validate.py` runs the data checks in §9.C.
7. `68-build-report.py` computes every number and chooses every representative ID from the data at build time. It **refuses to write** if any quotation is not found verbatim in the review it is attributed to, and asserts every trend direction stated in the narrative. The authenticity marks in §9.H are computed here by rule, not by hand.
8. `68-verify-report.py` re-reads the written file and checks it against the corpus (§9.M).

**Classification is hand judgement, not an algorithm.** No clustering, keyword rule or model assigned a code.

## 1.5 Signal thresholds

| Share of reviews | Label |
|---|---|
| < 0.1% | Ignore by default |
| 0.1% – < 0.5% | Weak signal |
| 0.5% – < 1% | Emerging signal |
| 1% – < 3% | Meaningful signal |
| 3% – 5% | Very strong signal |
| > 5% | High-priority signal |

At n=4,511, 0.1% ≈ 5 reviews, 1% ≈ 45 and 5% ≈ 226. Segment rates (within a star band, era, storefront or the paid segment) are labelled as segment rates and show both denominators; their band column is a segment band, not a corpus band.

## 1.6 Limitations, stated plainly

- **Selection.** People write when charged unexpectedly and when prompted. Neither group measures typical use.
- **Inflation.** E5 carries mechanical signs of a coordinated 5★ campaign (§9.H). Global praise shares are therefore upper bounds; §7.6 gives the figures without E5.
- **Purchases are unverifiable.** Charges, amounts and cancellation attempts are the reviewer's account.
- **No version data.** Product changes are dated from reviewer statements and bursts.
- **Language mix.** 2,171 reviews are in English; 24 other languages carry 2,340.
- **Thin early eras.** E1 has 104 reviews and E2 196; single days can move their shares.

# PART 2 — PRODUCT AND MONETIZATION MODEL

## 2.1 What the product is, from the reviews

A habit tracker branded as self-improvement and biohacking. Users pick habits from a library of explained habits (sleep, focus, mood, energy), check them off daily, rate their day, get reminders, follow prebuilt routines and see statistics. There is a journal, sleep tools, courses and "mind movies", and, in 2026, AI-assisted plans. Most reviewers did not arrive through the App Store: a social-media ad leads to a web questionnaire and a paid plan (a week, four weeks, twelve weeks, a quit-drinking or quit-smoking course), paid by card, Apple Pay or PayPal, followed by add-on offers. The app is then downloaded to use what was bought. Premium is also sold in the app.

| Capability | Positive evidence | Friction evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Habit tracking, check-ins, day rating | `PR_TRACKING` 156, `PR_EASY` 490, `PR_ACCOUNTABILITY` 128 | `BUG_TRACK` 13, `NEG_SIMPLE` 97, `REQ_REORDER` 7, `REQ_SCHEDULE` 5 |
| Habit library, explanations, sources | `PR_LIBRARY` 96, `PR_SCIENCE` 15, `PR_BIOHACK` 11 | `NEG_CONTENT` 33, `NEG_LIBRARY` 2, `NEG_BIOHACK_HYPE` 2 |
| Reminders, routines, organising the day | `PR_REMINDERS` 193, `PR_ROUTINES` 191, `PR_ORGANIZE` 194 | `BUG_REMINDER` 4, `NEG_NOTIF` 3, `NEG_OVERWHELM` 18 |
| Stats and insights, score, streaks | `PR_INSIGHTS` 91, `PR_GAMIFY` 40, `PR_STREAKS` 7 | `BUG_STATS` 1, `REQ_STATS` 1, `NEG_GAMIFY_UNCLEAR` 1 |
| Onboarding questionnaire, personalised plan, AI | `PR_ONBOARDING` 41, `PR_PERSONALIZED` 69, `PR_AI` 20 | `NEG_AI_POOR` 23, `NEG_AI_CONTENT` 8, `NEG_ONBOARDING_LONG` 7, `REQ_ONBOARDING` 9 |
| Journal, sleep, courses | `PR_JOURNAL` 38, `PR_SLEEP` 25, `PR_COURSES` 29 | `MON_ADDON` 42, `MON_NOT_DELIVERED` 17, `BUG_VIDEO` 1 |

- Public mean exceeds corpus mean in 12 of 20 storefronts; the exceptions (kz, fr, de, es, pl, it, il, ng) are storefronts whose corpus is dominated by E5 5★ or, for ng, praise. Public counts may include ratings without text and are not comparable over time.

## 2.3 Free / paid / trial / unclear — classified

| Capability or offer | Classification | Basis |
|---|---|---|
| Habit library, tracker, reminders, basic routines and stats | **Free** | listing; `PR_FREE` 26; "I find the basic non-paid version to be plenty" (#4001, `13973006077`, hu, 4★) |
| Premium (unlimited habits, deeper stats, premium routines, sleep tools) | **Paid in-app subscription, Monthly/Yearly** | listing; `MON_PAYWALL` 34 |
| Web plans (1-week, 4-week, 12-week, personal plan) | **Paid outside the App Store; reviewers report automatic conversion to a recurring charge** | `MKT_WEB` 32, `MON_PLAN` 19, `MON_WEB_BILLING` 303, `MON_RENEWAL_SHOCK` 227 |
| Add-on courses and programs (quit drinking/smoking, VIP training, packs) | **Paid add-ons, often offered right after payment** | `MON_ADDON` 42, `MON_ONBOARD_SELL` 37 |
| Lifetime access | **Sold 2021–2023; a Sweatcoin promotion in 2022; given free as compensation or retention in 2026** | `MON_LIFETIME` 15 (2021-09-11 → 2026-08-02), `MKT_SWEATCOIN` 2; #92, #3857, #4019 |
| Trial | **Free 7-day trial in-app; paid intro trials on the web** | `MON_TRIAL` 190, `MON_TRIAL_CHARGED` 19 |

## 2.4 What could not be established

- The web price list, the renewal terms shown at checkout, and whether a second confirmation is shown before an add-on charge.
- Whether the Monthly/Yearly Premium in the listing and the web subscriptions are the same product.
- Who wrote the E5 reviews. The marks in §9.H are consistent with a campaign; they do not identify its source.
- Refund rates. Reviewers report refunds granted and refused; the corpus cannot give a rate.

The mechanism reviewers name is low friction: a habit takes seconds to log, reminders prompt it, and the library explains why it matters. "I can easily set reminders and review my data" (#338, `10980045764`, us, 5★). "I love the reminders" (#26, `7196112058`, us, 5★). Explanations with sources are valued: "large library of habits plus corresponding sources" (#129, `9469982623`, de, 4★). Several call it gentler than other trackers (`PR_GRACE` 75): "doesn't make me feel bad when I mess up" (#1331, `12665119947`, us, 5★).

**How reviewers try to stop it.** `MON_ESCALATE` 189: blocking or cancelling the card, bank disputes and chargebacks, reports to Apple, police or regulators, lawyers. "raised a dispute" (#1621, `12757693206`, gb, 1★); "со своим юристом" *(with my lawyer)* (#478, `11808696317`, ua, 1★). Others advise readers how to block payments (`REV_TIP` 5): "Кому тоже нужно отменить подписку - зайдите в приложение вашего банка" *(For anyone who also needs to cancel the subscription — go into your bank app)* (#613, `12192255446`, kz, 2★).

**Charges after cancelling or deleting.** `MON_CHARGE_AFTER_CANCEL` **226** reviews, **5.01%** of 4,511 — High-priority signal, mean 1.14★, 2023-08-16 → 2026-09-04: "receiving confirmation from support that subscription was cancelled, they will continue charging my card" (#4067, `14043833740`, fr, 1★). `PRIV_DATA` 18 reviewers treat the stored card as the risk: "must delete your credit card after" (#4194, `14149306951`, ro, 1★).

**Legal framing.** `MON_LEGAL_CITE` **2** reviews, **0.04%** of 4,511: "comme le précise la loi" *(as the law requires)* (#1721, `12780273120`, fr, 1★).

| `MON_LIFETIME` | Lifetime access | 15 | 0.33% | Weak signal | 1.60 | 2021-09-11 → 2026-08-02 |
| `MKT_SWEATCOIN` | Sweatcoin promotion | 2 | 0.04% | Ignore by default | 1.00 | 2022-11-04 → 2022-12-01 |
| `NEG_NOT_AS_ADVERTISED` | Not what was advertised | 59 | 1.31% | Meaningful signal | 1.27 | 2021-02-28 → 2026-08-22 |
| `NEG_DARK_PATTERN` | Confirmshaming decline button | 1 | 0.02% | Ignore by default | 1.00 | 2026-08-21 → 2026-08-21 |

The upsell complaint predates the billing wave: "they will not honor your request immediately but instead apply pressure to get you to stay" (#62, `8944420573`, us, 1★) (2022). Buying "lifetime" or "premium" did not end the selling: "I bought a lifetime premium account but I don't get everything" (#75, `9053073549`, ca, 1★); "To do that, you have to pay yet another $40" (#154, `9506831573`, us, 1★). Reviewers who reached the app through a Sweatcoin promotion in 2022 were then asked to pay (#92, #105).

Silence is the most common support complaint: "No one ever responds to my emails" (#4296, `14284289709`, au, 1★). Replies that seem automated are `SUP_AI_SUSPECT` **5** reviews, **0.11%** of 4,511: "the bot responds with nonsense" (#4401, `14387826137`, rs, 1★). In E6 the pattern shifts to fast, named, generous replies (§0.4), including refunds reported in 1★ reviews that stay 1★ (#1201).

Defects are a small share of the corpus next to billing. Two defects matter: the **"Manage subscription" dead end** (`BUG_MANAGE_SUB` 60, §0.1) and the **launch failure of 2023-01-04**, when 13 reviews arrived in one day, 12 of them saying the app would not load: "App won't even open" (#124, `9456046627`, gb, 1★); "the app support link on the App Store also doesn't work" (#134, `9471340530`, na, 4★). Paid access that never unlocked is `BUG_PURCHASE` **18** reviews, **0.40%** of 4,511; login failures, often after a web purchase, are `BUG_LOGIN` **41** reviews, **0.91%** of 4,511.

The questionnaire promises a personal plan; reviewers who paid for it say the result is the same library: "No actual plan is provided" (#1596, `12750177813`, us, 1★). AI features are praised mostly in E6 (`PR_AI` 15 of 20), the same era in which reviewers object to AI-made design and text (`NEG_AI_CONTENT` 8). Language complaints are about mixed-language experiences: "La app está en español pero las notificaciones llegan en inglés" *(The app is in Spanish but notifications arrive in English)* (#122, `9452455789`, mx, 3★); "there are some language errors" (#3987, `13951278357`, pl, 5★). Two reviewers say the app is not designed for women (`NEG_INCLUSIVE`): "not designed with women in mind at all" (#313, `10845943293`, us, 1★).

Requests are few (`U_REQ_ANY` **89** reviews, **1.97%** of 4,511) because most reviewers are not using the app long enough to want more. The long-term users' asks are Apple Health integration and automatic tracking (`REQ_HEALTH` 9, `REQ_AUTO_TRACK` 2), habit ordering and schedules (`REQ_REORDER` 7, `REQ_SCHEDULE` 5), and a real free trial before any charge (`REQ_TRIAL` 4). "flagging a day as sick day" (#129, `9469982623`, de, 4★); "sort those habits chronologically" (#229, `10005041599`, us, 4★).

Leaving is rarely the story; being unable to leave is. Stated deletion (8) is outnumbered many times by escalation to banks (189). `REV_ME_TOO` **30** reviews, **0.67%** of 4,511 say they found the same complaint in other reviews first: "по отзывам- проблема частая" *(judging by the reviews, the problem is common)* (#4034, `14008782477`, kz, 1★).

| `MON_CANCEL_HARD` | Cannot cancel | 565 | 37.20% | High-priority signal | 13.12% |
| `MON_PAID` | States a purchase | 345 | 22.71% | High-priority signal | 8.69% |
| `SUP_NONE` | No reply | 336 | 22.12% | High-priority signal | 7.80% |
| `MON_WEB_BILLING` | Billed outside App Store | 286 | 18.83% | High-priority signal | 6.72% |
| `MON_RENEWAL_SHOCK` | Cheap plan becomes large recurring charge | 217 | 14.29% | High-priority signal | 5.03% |
| `MON_CHARGE_AFTER_CANCEL` | Charged after cancelling | 215 | 14.15% | High-priority signal | 5.01% |
| `MON_REFUND` | Asks for a refund | 203 | 13.36% | High-priority signal | 4.90% |
| `MON_ESCALATE` | Bank, card block, dispute, report | 185 | 12.18% | High-priority signal | 4.19% |
| `MON_TRIAL` | Trial mentioned | 171 | 11.26% | High-priority signal | 4.21% |
| `MON_REFUND_FAIL` | Refund refused or ignored | 117 | 7.70% | High-priority signal | 2.66% |

**Reading.** 5★ reviews are generic praise and ease of use (1,767 of 2,693 are from E5). 4★ reviews are the most informative praise: specific features plus a paywall or upsell caveat. 3★ and 2★ reviews pair a usable tracker with a price, upsell or content objection. 1★ reviews are billing: 89.7% of them carry a billing code.

## 4.3 Rating-versus-text contradictions

`CONTRA_RATING` **37** reviews, **0.82%** of 4,511. 28 are 4–5★ reviews whose text reports a problem, most often an unwanted charge: "Cancele y me siguen cobrando" *(I cancelled and they keep charging me)* (#2575, `13024030429`, mx, 5★). 8 are 1–2★ reviews whose text is praise: "it is easy to use, has a great design" (#4100, `14061204433`, lb, 1★). Mexico is over-represented (8 of 37). Two reviewers explain a star as tactical (`REV_STAR_TACTIC`): "Le puse 1 estrella porque si no no puedo publicarlo" *(I gave it 1 star because otherwise I cannot post it)* (#4415, `14402512848`, cl, 1★). All are kept and coded by their text.

**All contradiction IDs:** `9268005691`, `9471340530`, `9471602344`, `9471782298`, `9916363042`, `10070542789`, `10352151908`, `10728484611`, `10897773819`, `11124000123`, `11498657504`, `11660932242`, `12252966466`, `12363699179`, `12449215865`, `12627031967`, `12900124365`, `13024030429`, `13034690787`, `13052130994`, `13083762123`, `13085508391`, `13231608081`, `13369168247`, `13390247273`, `13495054016`, `13517278706`, `13556601920`, `13682167647`, `13778471383`, `13885873948`, `14061204433`, `14074456444`, `14239062307`, `14381989263`, `14391219097`, `14411183250`.

# PART 5 — PAID-USER AND UPGRADE ANALYSIS

## 5.1 The populations, and what they are not

- **Stated purchase, charge or refund: `U_PAID` 1280 (28.38% of 4,511)**, mean 1.19★. Of these, 392 state a deliberate purchase (`MON_PAID`), 1070 describe a charge they did not agree to (`U_UNAGREED_CHARGE`).
- **Stated intent: `MON_WILL_PAY` 16 (0.35%)**, mean 4.50★.
- **Stated barrier: `U_PAYWALL` 53 (1.17%)** plus price objection `MON_PRICE` 76.

**None of these is a conversion rate.** The corpus cannot say how many users pay, renew or are refunded.

## 5.2 What triggers a purchase

Within the 392 reviews that state a deliberate purchase (segment rates):

1. **An ad and a cheap intro plan**: `MKT_AD` 43 (11.0%), `MKT_WEB` 19, `MON_TRIAL` 33 (8.4%), `MON_PLAN` 19. "I signed up for this app via an instagram ad and paid £7 for one course" (#3818, `13756475547`, gb, 1★).
2. **A specific problem sold by the ad**: `USE_ADDICTION` 5, `MON_ADDON` 37 (§0.5).
3. **Liking the free tracker, rarely**: only 2 of the 408 reviews that purchased or intend to also praise the free version; the free tier is seldom the stated path to paying. "definitely considering the premium version" (#229, `10005041599`, us, 4★); "So great that I bought lifetime access" (#3874, `13829512466`, us, 1★).

## 5.4 What purchasers report

331 of 392 purchasers (84.4%) carry a billing complaint; 26 (6.6%) rate 4–5★. The satisfied ones are a minority and name value: "Money well spent" (#82, `9210591393`, us, 5★); "Рекомендую, оно того стоит" *(I recommend it, it is worth it)* (#3576, `13552868680`, ua, 5★). Value-for-money statements (`MON_WORTH` 32) split both ways.

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 4,511 | Band | Mean ★ | Dates |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|---|
| `MON_PAID` | States a purchase | 392 | 8.69% | High-priority signal | 1.32 | 2021-02-25 → 2026-09-06 |
| `MON_WORTH` | Value-for-money judgement | 32 | 0.71% | Emerging signal | 4.59 | 2022-10-22 → 2026-07-18 |
| `MON_TIER_TRAP` | Paid tier still gated | 24 | 0.53% | Emerging signal | 1.12 | 2022-09-05 → 2026-08-02 |
| `MON_NOT_DELIVERED` | Paid item never delivered | 17 | 0.38% | Weak signal | 1.24 | 2022-04-17 → 2026-03-31 |
| `BUG_PURCHASE` | Purchase not credited | 18 | 0.40% | Weak signal | 1.39 | 2021-07-30 → 2025-12-09 |
| `MON_LIFETIME` | Lifetime access | 15 | 0.33% | Weak signal | 1.60 | 2021-09-11 → 2026-08-02 |
| `MON_REFUND_OK` | Refund received | 27 | 0.60% | Emerging signal | 3.56 | 2022-05-28 → 2026-08-27 |
| `MON_REFUND_PARTIAL` | Partial refund | 4 | 0.09% | Ignore by default | 1.50 | 2025-06-02 → 2026-05-13 |
| `MON_REFUND_FAIL` | Refund refused or ignored | 120 | 2.66% | Meaningful signal | 1.06 | 2021-02-28 → 2026-08-29 |

- **Paid, received nothing**: "I bought my personal habit plan but I didn't receive it" (#37, `8577952232`, mx, 1★); "l never received the plan or any confirmation" (#3951, `13908127228`, ua, 1★).
- **Paid, then asked to pay again**: "After subscribing, lets you PAY AGAIN" (#221, `9953724056`, ph, 1★).
- **Partial refund after dispute**: "They returned the first payment" (#1621, `12757693206`, gb, 1★).

## 5.5 Upgrade barriers among non-buyers

| Code | Meaning | n | % of 4,511 | Band | Mean ★ | Dates |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---:|---|
| `MON_PAYWALL` | Features behind paywall | 34 | 0.75% | Emerging signal | 3.56 | 2022-05-16 → 2026-09-04 |
| `MON_PRICE` | Too expensive | 76 | 1.68% | Meaningful signal | 1.70 | 2022-02-16 → 2026-09-02 |
| `MON_HARD_PAYWALL` | Nothing usable without paying | 7 | 0.16% | Weak signal | 1.29 | 2020-11-05 → 2026-08-18 |
| `MON_FREE_LIMIT` | Free version too limited | 5 | 0.11% | Weak signal | 3.20 | 2023-06-24 → 2026-07-29 |
| `REQ_FREE_TIER` | More free features | 6 | 0.13% | Weak signal | 3.67 | 2026-01-30 → 2026-08-18 |
| `REQ_TRIAL` | A real free trial | 4 | 0.09% | Ignore by default | 3.50 | 2023-06-24 → 2026-08-13 |
| `MON_TRIAL_OBJECTION` | Won't pay without trying | 6 | 0.13% | Weak signal | 2.83 | 2023-06-24 → 2026-08-26 |
| `MON_AFFORD` | Hardship | 11 | 0.24% | Weak signal | 1.82 | 2023-03-18 → 2026-02-22 |
| `MON_PRICE_UNCLEAR` | Price unclear | 4 | 0.09% | Ignore by default | 2.25 | 2025-07-15 → 2026-03-10 |
| `MON_CONFUSED` | Subscription confusion | 14 | 0.31% | Weak signal | 1.57 | 2025-04-11 → 2026-06-30 |
| `MON_WILL_PAY` | Would pay / recommends premium | 16 | 0.35% | Weak signal | 4.50 | 2023-01-12 → 2026-09-04 |

- **Everything worthwhile is paid**: "Basically no features are free" (#39, `8677014043`, ca, 1★); "pour accéder au compte rendu fallait directement l'abonnent" *(to access the report you needed the subscription straight away)* (#4442, `14442549388`, fr, 3★).
- **No trial before paying**: "it would be perfect if they had a trial version instead of direct monthly payments" (#4265, `14244482841`, fr, 4★).
- **Cannot afford it**: "ich hab halt nicht das Geld um mir ein Abo zu kaufen" *(I just don't have the money to buy a subscription)* (#425, `11430037786`, de, 4★).
- **The price is unclear**: "it takes forever to figure out how much it costs a month" (#2086, `12895105557`, us, 1★).

## 5.6 Refund and churn drivers after purchase

Refund requests: `MON_REFUND` **221** reviews, **4.90%** of 4,511 — Very strong signal, mean 1.17★, 2021-02-28 → 2026-08-06. Reported outcomes: refused or ignored `MON_REFUND_FAIL` 120, received `MON_REFUND_OK` 27 (18 of them in E6), partial `MON_REFUND_PARTIAL` 4. The driver is not dissatisfaction with the app but a charge the reviewer did not expect: 154 of 221 refund requests (69.7%) carry `U_UNAGREED_CHARGE`.

## 5.7 What cannot be claimed here

- The share of users who pay, the share of payers who are charged unexpectedly, or refund rates.
- Whether the web and in-app subscriptions are priced or renewed on the same terms.
- Whether the 2026 refunds reflect a policy change or selective handling of reviewers.

## 6.2 Eligibility

Storefronts with **≥50 reviews** support standalone claims: us (724), ua (474), kz (249), gb (231), fr (227), de (177), es (170), ca (164), pl (161), mx (147), it (138), au (117), co (105), by (95), il (73), br (61), ar (58), cl (57), ng (55), ae (50). Each gets a section below. Theme tables show storefront segment rates against the global rate; lift = storefront rate ÷ global rate. "Distinctive" themes have lift ≥ 1.8 and at least 5 reviews in the storefront. Storefronts below 50 are in §6.24 as limited evidence.

**Reading:** The US is the largest storefront (16.05% of the corpus) and the one with the longest history: 285 reviews predate 2025-04. It carries most of the upsell complaint (`MON_UPSELL` 33 of 80) and of the AI/personalisation criticism (`NEG_AI_POOR` 8 of 23). The two most-voted reviews in the corpus, #228 and #230 (19 votes each, June 2023), are short US tracker praise. Outside E5 the US mean is 3.39★ against a public 4.33★.

| `PR_GENERIC` | General praise | 4 | 6.9% | 9.8% | 0.71× |

**Distinctive:** `PR_ORGANIZE` 7 (2.81×), `PR_MOTIVATION` 5 (2.09×), `PR_RECOMMEND` 5 (1.87×).

**Reading:** 50 of 58 reviews are in Spanish. Organising and motivation praise are over-represented (`PR_ORGANIZE` 7, `PR_MOTIVATION` 5). #2123 states the bait price precisely, and #194 cannot afford the app from Argentina.

> "Ofrecen 4 semanas a 0.35 usd la semana y resulta que cobran 9.92usd por 7 días" — #2123, `12910718578`, ar, 1★
> *(translation: They offer 4 weeks at 0.35 USD a week and it turns out they charge 9.92 USD for 7 days)*

## 6.23 High-spend and high-review-volume markets

**No consumer-spend dataset was fetched in this session**, so no spend ranking is asserted. Two groups are defined from captured sources:

- **High-spend proxy group** = the six eligible storefronts with the most public ratings in Apple's lookup API (an install-base proxy, not spend): us (3,944), ua (840), mx (776), gb (645), ca (507), au (445).
- **High-review-volume group** = storefronts with ≥150 reviews in this corpus: us, ua, kz, gb, fr, de, es, ca, pl.

| Group | Reviews | Mean ★ | Mean ★ outside E5 | Praise % | Billing % | Web billing % | Support complaint % | E5 share % |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| High-spend proxy: us + ua + mx + gb + ca + au | 1,857 | 3.50 | 3.04 | 54.6% | 32.4% | 7.3% | 12.1% | 48.5% |
| High-volume: us + ua + kz + gb + fr + de + es + ca + pl | 2,577 | 3.69 | 3.08 | 58.9% | 28.4% | 6.7% | 10.0% | 54.0% |
| us only | 724 | 3.73 | 3.39 | 58.7% | 24.9% | 3.7% | 9.7% | 41.9% |
| ua + kz + by (Russian/Ukrainian/Kazakh-speaking) | 818 | 3.28 | 2.39 | 48.4% | 41.2% | 12.7% | 13.8% | 60.0% |
| mx + co + ar + cl + br (Latin America) | 428 | 3.47 | 2.85 | 51.2% | 31.5% | 4.9% | 12.1% | 50.9% |
| Global | 4,511 | 3.55 | 3.02 | 55.6% | 31.8% | 6.7% | 11.5% | 52.4% |

**Reading:** the billing complaint is highest in the ua + kz + by group outside E5 (61.5% of its 327 non-E5 reviews) against 29.9% for the US outside E5. The high-spend proxy group's corpus mean hides a split: E5 lifts it, and the non-E5 mean is lower in every group.

# PART 7 — TIME-TREND ANALYSIS

## 7.1 Method

Eras are cut where monthly volume, mean rating and reviewer-described events change together. The monthly series was inspected before the era tables were computed; §9.C asserts the boundaries' consistency with dated events.

- **E1 → E2 at 2022-12-01**: first sustained rise in volume and the 2023-01-04 launch failure.
- **E2 → E3 at 2024-01-01**: a calendar cut after the first reviews describing web billing (from 2023-09) and charges after cancelling (from 2023-08).
- **E3 → E4 at 2024-12-01**: monthly volume doubles (27 in 2024-11, 54 in 2024-12) and the mean falls below 2★ for two months (1.89★, 1.65★).
- **E4 → E5 at 2025-04-01**: monthly volume jumps from the low hundreds to 234–536 and the mean to about 4★.
- **E5 → E6 at 2025-10-01**: volume falls back and the mean drops to 1.93★ in 2025-10.

| Era | Name | Dates | Reviews | Per month | Mean ★ | 1–2★ % | 5★ % |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| E1 | Early paid model | 2020-05-12 → 2022-11-30 | 104 | 5 | 3.34 | 39.4% | 54.8% |
| E2 | Launch failure, tier traps | 2022-12-01 → 2023-12-31 | 196 | 15 | 2.42 | 58.2% | 19.4% |
| E3 | Web billing appears | 2024-01-04 → 2024-11-29 | 215 | 20 | 2.99 | 47.4% | 41.9% |
| E4 | Billing collapse | 2024-12-01 → 2025-03-31 | 395 | 99 | 2.23 | 68.6% | 28.6% |
| E5 | Five-star wave | 2025-04-01 → 2025-09-30 | 2,363 | 394 | 4.03 | 24.0% | 74.8% |
| E6 | Named agents, addiction ads | 2025-10-01 → 2026-09-06 | 1,238 | 103 | 3.34 | 38.4% | 50.7% |

## 7.4 What changed

**E1 (early paid model, 104 reviews, 3.34★).** Reviews are sparse and split. Lifetime access is sold and upsold (`MON_LIFETIME` 3, `MON_TIER_TRAP` 1), a Sweatcoin promotion brings users who are then charged (#92), and the in-app upsell complaint begins (#62). The first long, identical 5★ texts under different names appear in 2022-10 (#84, #86, #90).

**E2 (launch failure and tier traps, 196 reviews, 2.42★).** On 2023-01-04, 13 reviews arrive, 12 saying the app will not load; `BUG_LAUNCH` reaches 10.7% of E2, its peak. Paying and then finding more paywalls peaks too (`MON_TIER_TRAP` 4.6%). The first web-billing and charge-after-cancel reviews appear in 2023-08/09.

**E3 (web billing appears, 215 reviews, 2.99★).** `MON_WEB_BILLING` rises to 10.7% and `MON_RENEWAL_SHOCK` to 8.4%. Praise is still substantial (45.1%), and #494 (2024-11) is a reworded copy of #185 (2023).

**E4 (billing collapse, 395 reviews, 2.23★).** The lowest mean of any era. Billing complaints reach 63.0% of reviews and web billing 15.9%, both peaks. The "Manage subscription" dead end is first reported (2025-03-14). Ukrainian, Belarusian and Kazakh storefronts become major sources (151 of 395 E4 reviews).

**E5 (five-star wave, 2,363 reviews, 4.03★).** Volume multiplies and the mean rises above 4★ while billing complaints continue (536 reviews, 22.7%). 320 E5 reviews (13.5%) carry at least one authenticity mark (§9.H). Support that answers cancellations with offers is described most in this era in absolute terms (`SUP_RETENTION` 38).

**E6 (named agents and addiction ads, 1,238 reviews, 3.34★).** The wave stops in 2025-10; that month's mean is 1.93★. Support praise rises to 5.0% with named agents and refunds (`MON_REFUND_OK` 18 of 27); two texts show reviews being requested (#3539, #4007). Addiction-themed ad buyers arrive (`USE_ADDICTION` 23 of 24), AI features are both praised and resented, and the $57 post-payment add-on is still reported (#4306, #4369).

## 7.5 What persisted

- **The unrequested extra charge**, `MON_EXTRA_CHARGE` from 2021-02-28 → 2026-09-06.
- **No way to cancel in the app**, `MON_CANCEL_HARD` from 2023-01-09 → 2026-08-30.
- **Upsells inside the app**, `MON_UPSELL` from 2021-12-20 → 2026-08-22.
- **Praise for ease of use**, `PR_EASY` in every era (6.1–18.1%).
- **"Just a checklist"**, `NEG_SIMPLE` from 2021-09-11 → 2026-08-21.

## 7.6 Sensitivity — do the bursts drive the conclusions?

The three busiest days (2025-09-23 (42), 2025-08-29 (33), 2025-08-31 (33)) hold 108 reviews, all in E5. The table removes E5 entirely, then removes only reviews carrying an authenticity mark (355), then shows E6 alone.

| Headline share | All 4,511 | Without E5 (2,148) | Without marked reviews (4,156) | E6 only (1,238) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `U_PRAISE_ANY` | 55.6% | 47.3% | 53.1% | 55.9% |
| `U_OUTCOME` | 11.5% | 9.3% | 11.1% | 11.7% |
| `PR_EASY` | 10.9% | 11.9% | 10.7% | 13.3% |
| `U_BILLING_COMPLAINT` | 31.8% | 41.8% | 34.4% | 37.9% |
| `MON_SCAM` | 16.6% | 21.3% | 17.9% | 21.1% |
| `MON_WEB_BILLING` | 6.7% | 8.6% | 7.3% | 7.6% |
| `MON_CANCEL_HARD` | 13.1% | 16.0% | 14.2% | 14.7% |

Removing only the marked reviews barely moves the headlines, because the marks catch a minority of E5 (320 of 2,363). Removing E5 moves praise from 55.6% to 47.3% and billing from 31.8% to 41.8%. Every Part 0 finding on billing, the funnel and support holds in both the full corpus and E6 alone.

# PART 8 — PRODUCT IMPLICATIONS

Each item cites the findings it rests on. **Immediate fixes** address harms already reported; **experiments** test a hypothesis; **research questions** need data the corpus cannot give.

## 8.1 Immediate fix — a cancel button that works for every subscription (§0.1, §3.3)

- Route "Manage subscription" to wherever the user's subscription is actually billed (web, Apple Pay, PayPal, card), and add in-app cancellation for web subscriptions (`BUG_MANAGE_SUB` 60, `MON_CANCEL_HARD` 592, `MON_EMAIL` 13).
- Confirm cancellation immediately and stop billing on that confirmation (`MON_CHARGE_AFTER_CANCEL` 226; #4067, #4504).

## 8.2 Immediate fix — no charge without an explicit second confirmation (§0.2, §3.4)

- Remove one-tap add-on charges after the first payment (`MON_ONBOARD_SELL` 37; the $57 pattern in 58 reviews), and replace shaming decline copy with a neutral "No thanks" (#4450).
- Show the renewal price and date on the intro-plan screen and in a receipt email (`MON_RENEWAL_SHOCK` 227, `MON_RECEIPT` 17, `MON_BAIT` 55).

## 8.3 Immediate fix — honour the first cancellation or refund request (§0.4, §3.5)

- Process the request before making any retention offer (`SUP_RETENTION` 85); reply to every billing email (`SUP_NONE` 352). The 2026 named-agent replies show this is possible; it should not depend on reaching a named agent.

## 8.4 Immediate fix — stop requesting and templating reviews (§0.3, §0.4, §9.H)

- Stop asking support contacts for reviews (#4007) and any posting schedule (#3539). The E5 marks (320 reviews) put the public rating's credibility at risk; reviewers already distrust it (`REV_DISTRUST` 8).

## 8.5 Immediate fix — ads that match the product and the language (§0.5, §0.7)

- Addiction-themed ads should lead to a product that addresses the addiction, or not run (`USE_ADDICTION` 24, `NEG_NOT_AS_ADVERTISED` 59).
- Do not advertise in a language the content is not in (`NEG_LANG` 32; #687, #650); declare and localise Ukrainian and Kazakh if those storefronts are targeted.

## 8.6 Experiment — a real free trial before any charge (§5.5)

- Replace paid intro plans with a free trial that ends without charge unless confirmed. Hypothesis: fewer disputes (`MON_ESCALATE` 189) and refund requests (`MON_REFUND` 221) without fewer committed subscribers (`MON_WILL_PAY` 16, `REQ_TRIAL` 4). Measure chargeback rate and 90-day retention.

## 8.7 Experiment — make the paid plan visibly different from the free tracker (§0.7, §3.7)

- Test a plan that is demonstrably personal (schedule, sequence, check-ins that adapt) against the current library. Hypothesis: lowers `NEG_SIMPLE` (97), `NEG_AI_POOR` (23) and `MON_NOT_DELIVERED` (17).

## 8.8 Experiment — Apple Health integration and habit ordering (§3.8)

- The long-term users' requests: `REQ_HEALTH` 9, `REQ_AUTO_TRACK` 2, `REQ_REORDER` 7, `REQ_SCHEDULE` 5. Hypothesis: raises retention among free users who already value the tracker (§0.6).

## 8.9 What the corpus says to keep

- The simple check-off tracker, reminders and routines (§0.6, §3.2).
- The habit library with explanations and sources (§3.2).
- A useful free tier; reviewers who pay often start there (§5.2).
- Fast, human support that resolves billing problems (§0.4), without the review requests.

## 8.10 Research questions the corpus cannot answer

1. What share of web purchasers are charged an add-on or renewal they later dispute, and at what chargeback rate?
2. How many active subscribers are billed outside the App Store, and can they see or cancel that subscription in the app?
3. Who produced the E5 reviews, and how were the E6 named-agent reviews requested?
4. Do addiction-ad cohorts retain or refund differently from other cohorts?
5. What is satisfaction among free users who never saw a paid funnel?

# PART 9 — EVIDENCE APPENDIX

## 9.A Counting rules

1. **Denominator.** Global = 4,511. Star band, era, storefront, purchaser and outside-E5 denominators are stated at each use.
2. **Non-exclusive.** A review is counted once in each theme it carries. Percentages sum past 100%.
3. **Codes come from the text, never from the star rating.** A 5★ whose text is a charge complaint is coded as a charge complaint and flagged `CONTRA_RATING`.
4. **`MON_PAID` requires a statement of a deliberate purchase.** A charge the reviewer did not agree to is `MON_EXTRA_CHARGE`, `MON_RENEWAL_SHOCK`, `MON_CHARGE_AFTER_CANCEL` or `MON_TRIAL_CHARGED`; `MON_SCAM` records the reviewer's word (scam, fraud, thieves) and is not a finding of fraud.
5. **`MON_WEB_BILLING` requires the reviewer to say the charge or subscription is not in Apple subscriptions, or was made on the web / Apple Pay / PayPal / card.**
6. **`OUT_*` outcomes are first-person.** Anticipation is `JUST_STARTED`.
7. **Support-agent codes record a name in the text** (`SUP_NICK` for Nick, `SUP_NAMED_AGENT` for others), with any sentiment; praise is `PR_SUPPORT`.
8. **Authenticity is never hand-coded.** Only textual evidence is coded (`REV_COPIED` when copying is verified against another review, `REV_SOLICITED_LEAK` when the text says the review was requested). Name, look-alike-letter and duplicate-body marks are computed by rule (§9.H) and are never used to drop reviews.
9. **Unions are derived, never hand-applied**; mechanical codes (`STAR_n`, `ERA_En`) come from the record.
10. **Signal bands** are computed from count ÷ denominator using §1.5.

| Union | Meaning | Members | n |
|---|---|---|---:|
| `U_PRAISE_ANY` | Any praise code | `PR_GENERIC`, `PR_EASY`, `PR_DESIGN`, `PR_RECOMMEND`, `PR_ORGANIZE`, `PR_REMINDERS`, `PR_ROUTINES`, `PR_MOTIVATION`, `PR_TRACKING`, `PR_ACCOUNTABILITY`, `PR_LIBRARY`, `PR_INSIGHTS`, `PR_PERF`, `PR_GRACE`, `PR_PERSONALIZED`, `PR_CUSTOM`, `PR_ALLINONE`, `PR_BETTER_THAN`, `PR_BEST`, `PR_ONBOARDING`, `PR_GAMIFY`, `PR_JOURNAL`, `PR_SUPPORT`, `PR_COACH`, `PR_STICKY`, `PR_CONCEPT`, `PR_COURSES`, `PR_FREE`, `PR_SLEEP`, `PR_AI`, `PR_DEV`, `PR_SCIENCE`, `PR_BIOHACK`, `PR_STREAKS`, `PR_NO_ADS`, `PR_UPDATE`, `PR_LANG`, `PR_WATCH`, `PR_REFERRAL`, `PR_SCHEDULE` | 2508 |
| `U_OUTCOME` | Any positive outcome | `OUT_MENTAL`, `OUT_PRODUCTIVE`, `OUT_HABIT`, `OUT_LIFE`, `OUT_HEALTH`, `OUT_RESULT` | 520 |

### Mechanical codes

`STAR_1`…`STAR_5` from `rating`; `ERA_E1`…`ERA_E6` from `date` using the boundaries in §7.1.

## 9.C Validation — 989 checks, 0 failures

`68-validate.py` output, verbatim:

```
989 checks, 0 failures
  coverage: 9
  codes: 5
  theme_map: 633
  unions: 48
  mech: 12
  timeline: 7
  semantic: 10
  lang: 27
  bounds: 238
```

The timeline checks test that dated product events stay in their period: the Sweatcoin promotion only in 2022; the non-Nick named agents only from 2026; the "Manage subscription" dead end never before 2025; web billing never before 2023; review-request leaks only in E6; addiction-ad buyers at least 90% in E6; AI-content objections only in E6. The semantic checks require every 1–2★ review to carry a negative, request, refund/cancel, noise or contradiction code; every `BUG_MANAGE_SUB` to co-occur with web billing, a cancellation failure or an unagreed charge; every `SUP_RETENTION` to occur in a cancellation, refund, charge or support context; every 1★ review that received a refund to still carry its grievance.

### What earlier runs caught

1. **Paraphrased quotations.** Notes that quoted loosely were rewritten to the exact text (#27, #125, #133, #321, #353, #3942, #4192). #3942's source spells "falicita"; #4192 says "te ayuda", not "me ayuda".
2. **Fragments that matched anywhere.** One-letter fragments ("a", "Es") on #1175 and #1936 passed the substring check trivially and were removed.
3. **Placeholder codes.** Working codes were renamed or removed before the taxonomy was fixed (`MON_CHARGEBACK` → `MON_ESCALATE`, `REQ_CUSTOM_METRICS` → `REQ_CUSTOMIZE`; `REV_SUSPECT` dropped in favour of the mechanical tests in §9.H; stray `CONTRA_RATING_*` variants removed).
4. **Semantic checks corrected seven judgements**: a 2★ "Refunded" and a 5★ "Request for refund — Success" lacked the contradiction and refund codes (#4397, #4259); a 1★ whose only text is that the developer hides reviews needed `REV_CENSOR` (#271); a 1★ describing an upsell and a threat to leave was wrongly flagged `CONTRA_RATING` (#3874); a 1★ praising performance lacked `PR_PERF` (#3412); an inactive unsubscribe button lacked `MON_CANCEL_HARD` (#867); an evasive cancellation exchange lacked `MON_CANCEL` (#3632).
5. **An agent-name sweep** found six support-agent mentions without an agent code (#177, #2449, #2500, #3052, #3897, #4007) and two false hits that were left uncoded: #842, where "Nick" is the reviewer's own name, and #2301, where Polish "Sam" means "myself".
6. **Near-duplicate singletons** were merged (`BUG_SOUND` → `BUG_AUDIO`, `PR_CALENDAR` → `PR_SCHEDULE`; `MON_UPGRADE` dropped as already covered by `MON_PAID`).

### What validation cannot check

Whether a code is the *right* judgement. That is auditable only by reading the note on each line of `68-cls/`, which quotes the evidence.

Reading: the most-named amounts are 50 (66), 57 (53), 40 (42). Small intro amounts (about 5–10) cluster with a second larger amount in the same review: the plan plus the add-on (57) or the renewal (39.95, 49.93, 79, 87). Amounts in local currencies (hryvnia, roubles, pesos) are not captured by the pattern.

## 9.H Burst, solicitation and authenticity checks

All marks below are computed by rule at build time. They are disclosed, never used to remove reviews from any count.

**Look-alike-letter IDs:** #889 #1050 #1077 #1175 #1176 #1184 #1196 #1199 #1202 #1291 #1303 #1345 #1419 #1597 #1599 #1609 #1639 #1666 #1673 #1718 #1754 #1785 #1823 #1862 #1878 #1894 #1896 #1936 #2018 #2046 #2062 #2088 #2090 #2165 #2170 #2182 #2209 #2213 #2227 #2240 #2282 #2302 #2306 #2349 #2377 #2475 #2520 #2635 #2695 #2787 #2879 #2887 #2915 #2936 #2943 #2962 #2999 #3007 #3075 #3093.

**Reading:** the E5 wave carries every mechanical mark of coordinated reviewing (generated names, perturbed text, shared templates, one-line reviews in undeclared languages), concentrated in six months, with a step change in volume and mean rating at both ends. The marks catch 320 of 2,363 E5 reviews; the rest of the wave cannot be separated from organic reviews by rule and is treated as unverifiable. Separately, E6 shows solicitation through support contacts (#4007) and a posting instruction left in a review (#3539); the author of #3539, `Dedrooner`, posted a second 5★ review three weeks later on another storefront (#3717, de). Neither pattern identifies who wrote the reviews.

## 9.I Developer and support responsiveness — the evidence both ways

Developer replies are not in the dataset; `REV_DEV_REPLY` **8** reviews, **0.18%** of 4,511 refer to one. "The developer has refunded my payment as promised" (#4452, `14458892843`, th, 2★). Support praise: `PR_SUPPORT` **71** reviews, **1.57%** of 4,511 (62 in E6), e.g. "dokonał zwrotu" *(made a refund)* (#4472, `14477514213`, pl, 5★). Support complaints: `SUP_NONE` **352** reviews, **7.80%** of 4,511, `SUP_RETENTION` **85** reviews, **1.88%** of 4,511, `SUP_POOR` **84** reviews, **1.86%** of 4,511, e.g. "підтримка почала затягувати процес і намагатися переконати мене залишитися" *(support began dragging out the process and trying to persuade me to stay)* (#4477, `14485315763`, ua, 1★) (2026-08). Both kinds continue through the last month of the corpus.

## 9.L Reproduction

```
sh Temp/68-run-all.sh
```

Runs dump → reconcile → alignment check → build classification → validate → build report → verify, then build and verify again so §9.M carries the final verification output.
